### 2026-09-22 03:14:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Paraguay #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Paraguay Holds Key Interest Rate at 5.50% — Paraguay’s central bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 5.50% at its September 2026 meeting. The bank noted that economic activity continues to perform favorably, in line with its projected 4.5% growth for 2026. Annual inflation remains low, mainly due to the behavior of goods prices, which increased 0.4%, particularly non-energy goods. Meanwhile, inflation in services and rents remains around 3.5%. Inflation is projected at 3.3% by year-end, converging toward the target over the monetary policy horizon. Inflation expectations remain aligned with the target. The external environment remains subject to risks, including developments in international petroleum product prices and uncertainty related to the conflict in the Middle East, particularly its potential implications for supply conditions and energy prices. In addition, market expectations point to another increase in the federal funds rate over the remainder of the year.

【特朗普政府提議撥款50億美元啟動海灣能源設施重建投資基金】據美國和多位中東官員透露，特朗普政府已提議向一項新基金注資50億美元，以幫助中東國家重建在伊朗戰爭中遭到破壞的能源基礎設施，並降低這些國家對霍爾木茲海峽石油和天然氣運輸的依賴。此舉等於預設承認，這場長達七個月的衝突已經攪亂了全球能源市場，摧毀了該地區的輸油管道和煉油廠，而重建這些設施將耗費巨資。根據特朗普的計劃，美國將尋求八個中東夥伴國——沙烏地阿拉伯、阿聯酋、卡達、巴林、科威特、阿曼、伊拉克和約旦的等額配套資金，目標是成立一個規模達100億美元的投資基金。該投資基金將被命名為“盟國信任與建設夥伴關係”（Pact）。美國官員表示，關於該基金的討論正在進行中，相關安排條款可能會發生變化。目前尚不清楚其他國家何時（甚至是否會）正式加入。

### 2026-09-22 02:39:07  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【Fed's MUSALEM: Further rate hikes may be needed to curb inflation】Fed governor MUSALEM said on Monday that strong demand and commodity shocks beyond oil may require further rate increases to bring down inflation. He urged acting sooner rather than waiting, saying persistent demand and recurring supply pressures are increasing inflation risk and that, absent further policy action, inflation is likely to be materially above the 2% target in about 18 months. He said policy must meaningfully constrain inflation to allow the Fed to reach its target within roughly a year and a half and give time for tightening to work, adding that earlier, gradual tightening is preferable to later, larger and potentially abrupt moves. MUSALEM warned that inflation "is not a risk, it is here," and that underlying inflation excluding oil and other supply-related factors may be several percentage points above the Fed's target and is moving in the wrong direction.

### 2026-09-22 02:26:50  #MKT News #PARA.O #Impact bullish #WBD.O

【Paramount settles with California and 11 states, clears key hurdle for $110bln Warner Bros. Discovery merger】Paramount reached a settlement with California and 11 other states that had sued to block its $110 billion acquisition of Warner Bros. Discovery, removing a major state-level obstacle to the deal. The agreement, announced by the California attorney general, requires Paramount to increase U.S. film production—adding at least $300 million a year in domestic production spending—and to raise annual theatrical releases after closing. It mandates production of 30 films per year in the first two post-close years and 32 per year in the following three years, with at least four independent films annually and a minimum 20% of releases classified as tentpoles.

【波蘭尋求分階段將駐波美軍規模擴大至1.5萬人】波蘭國防部副部長斯坦尼斯瓦夫·弗齊亞特克21日在接受波蘭RMF廣播電臺採訪時表示，波蘭尋求在其境內部署約1.5萬名美軍士兵。 弗齊亞特克說，“我們談論的最終目標是1.5萬名士兵”，其中約4000至5000名美軍士兵將駐紮在永久軍事基地，另有1萬至1.1萬名士兵以輪換方式部署。 弗齊亞特克表示，駐波美軍規模將分階段擴大，預計2039年達到目標。目前，波蘭境內有約6500至7000名美軍士兵輪換駐紮。 美國總統特朗普17日表示，在波蘭建立永久軍事基地一事取得“重大進展”。美國目前正在對其駐歐美軍部署情況進行為期6個月的評估，預計將於今年年底前完成。(新華社)

TSX Edges Higher as Banks Rise — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged higher to trade around the 36,000 mark on Monday amid gains among major banks. Oil prices fell on hopes for diplomatic progress between the US and Iran. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian is expected to attend the UN General Assembly this week, and US President Trump said he would be open to meeting him. Yields eased as the halt in the oil rally cooled concerns over spreading inflationary pressures from energy costs, lending support to credit-sensitive shares. Major banks rose, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, Scotiabank, and CIBC advancing nearly 1.5%. BoC Governor Macklem’s emphasized in a speech that monetary policy cannot reverse tariffs or change gasoline prices, while noting that the BoC’s role is to assess whether these shocks will have lasting effects on inflation and economic growth. Energy shares fell on lower crude prices, Canadian Natural and Suncor shedmore than 2%. Miners were also mostly lower as gold prices eased, WPM lost about 2%.

【標準普爾：銀行對人工智慧的應用將很快影響它們信用評級】標普全球評級週一在一份報告中表示，未來幾年，銀行對人工智慧(AI)的使用將越來越影響其信用評級。這家信用評級機構表示，銀行最終受益還是受損將取決於其AI戰略的成熟度以及治理框架的穩健程度。標普稱：“各銀行在採用AI的速度、治理成熟度和運營準備程度方面存在差異，這意味著未來幾年，AI將越來越成為強化或削弱金融機構信用資質的因素。”標普6月對全球179家金融機構進行調查。受訪機構預計，今年AI可幫助削減最多4%的成本，到2028年這一幅度將擴大至6%-8%。約84%的機構表示，目前已將AI用於支援業務職能和自動化。由於監管和聲譽風險，不到三分之一的機構表示使用AI開發新產品和服務。

DAX Closes Higher — Frankfurt's DAX 40 extended early gains to close about 1.1% firmer at 25,575 on Monday, supported by lower oil prices and easing bond yields amid hopes of a diplomatic resolution to the Middle East conflict. ### 2026-09-21 23:25:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Colombia Trade Deficit Hits Historical Series High — Colombia posted a trade deficit of $3.28 billion in July 2026, widening from a $2.05 billion gap a year earlier and marking the widest deficit in the historical series, which began in 1980. Imports totaled $7.97 billion, up 22.9% year over year. Manufacturing imports rose 16.1%, driven by higher purchases of machinery and transport equipment (24%) and chemicals and related products (11%). Imports of agriculture, food, and beverages increased 26.3%, mainly due to higher purchases of food products and live animals (27.2%). Imports of fuels and extractive industry products surged 77.3%, driven by a 79.6% increase in purchases of petroleum, petroleum products, and related products. Exports totaled $4.69 billion, up 5.9%, mainly due to a 51.6% increase in the other sectors group, driven by higher exports of non-monetary gold. Fuels and extractive industry products accounted for 40% of total exports and rose 8.3%.

Domestically, investors awaited August consumer inflation data and the SARB’s policy decision, both due on September 23. Headline Inflation fell to 4.3% in July from 5% in June but is expected to pick up again in August after diesel-price adjustments. South Africa could be heading for a 25 bps rate hike this week, as higher oil prices and the Fed’s latest move strengthen the case for tighter monetary policy amid renewed inflation risks. The key concern is that inflation could remain above target for longer. That said, the SARB's decision remains uncertain, particularly after the central bank surprised markets and economists in July by keeping its repo rate unchanged at 7%. Policymakers will again face a delicate balancing act between containing renewed inflationary pressures and supporting weak domestic economic activity.

【油價暴漲衝擊中期選舉 留給美國共和黨的時間所剩無幾】美國共和黨議員已經沒有多少時間來安撫因物價問題而憤怒的選民，國會山上演相互推諉的最後一幕。很多人認為，該問題是共和黨在中期選舉中面臨的最大政治隱患。 柴油是卡車與農業機械的主要燃料，柴油價格暴漲對農村以及紅州衝擊尤為嚴重。在艾奧瓦州、內布拉斯加州等地，共和黨正陷入比預期更加艱難的選戰，力求保住席位。部分策略師與議員擔憂，如果選民將怒火發洩到共和黨候選人身上，該黨或將遭遇災難性的選舉結果。議員們提出多項設想，包括暫停聯邦汽油稅、實施燃料出口禁令，同時也承認相關舉措存在各類現實制約。這場始於2月的衝突，已然成為特朗普總統及其所屬政黨的軟肋。此前特朗普承諾不會再爆發無休止的海外戰爭，還曾許諾壓低物價。距離選舉到來，參議院僅剩兩週的會期，目前並沒有計劃就暫停汽油稅或是其他旨在壓低燃油價格的措施舉行投票。即便參議員透過相關法案，也很難快速送至總統案頭，因為共和黨掌控的眾議院已於上週休會。

### 2026-09-21 22:48:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gasoline Futures Decline — US gasoline futures fell below $3.50 a gallon after touching $3.53, its highest level since May 20th, as improving flows through the Strait of Hormuz and renewed diplomatic prospects eased immediate supply concerns. Fears over prolonged disruptions to Saudi Arabia's East-West pipeline also abated following reports the critical line could partially restart within days. However, as winter approaches and heating demand rises, refiners are shifting output toward higher-priced diesel, tightening gasoline markets. Persistent Ukrainian attacks on Russian refining infrastructure have further heightened supply worries, with Russia set to extend its diesel export ban beyond September. Amid these geopolitical tensions, the US national average for regular gasoline climbed to $4.48 a gallon on September 21st, bringing it closer to this year’s record high of $4.56 reached on May 21st.

Meanwhile, traders are also focused on this week’s summit between US President Donald Trump and Chinese President Xi Jinping, with markets looking for signs of progress towards easing trade tensions. Demand for Bitcoin has also strengthened. US spot Bitcoin ETFs recorded $433 million in net inflows on September 18, led by Fidelity and BlackRock.

【大摩：基差交易“消亡”被嚴重誇大】包括Eli Carter在內的摩根士丹利策略師表示，美國國債現券-期貨基差交易依然活躍且運轉良好。他們指出，基差交易在規模、構成和預期回報方面已發生變化，但並未消失。從久期調整後的風險口徑來看，該交易“幾乎沒有收縮”。交易總名義規模已從1月1.5萬億美元的峰值降至約1.2萬億美元。規模的縮減主要集中在曲線前端——具體為TU（2年期）和FV（5年期）期貨。與此形成對比的是，策略師表示，代表曲線最遠端的WN（超長期）頭寸同比增長了50%。隨著銀行監管放鬆的推進，交易商風險敞口增加的問題受到關注，這一敞口確實有所擴大，但“目前的轉移幅度仍然溫和，我們認為不太可能對交易商的中介業務構成實質性約束”。

Dollar Index Above 100 — The dollar index edged up to 100.3 on Monday, reaching its highest level in almost three months and extending last week’s gain of nearly 1.1%, as the greenback continued to benefit from a more hawkish stance from the Federal Reserve. The central bank raised the federal funds rate by 25bps last week and is expected to deliver another hike by year-end. Chicago Fed President Goolsbee said he remains open to the possibility that inflation will resume its decline towards the 2% target, but warned that interest rates may need to rise if that fails to materialise. Meanwhile, Minneapolis Fed President Kashkari said yesterday that inflation remains too high across all sectors of the economy, rather than being driven solely by oil prices. Oil prices however, fell for a fourth consecutive session amid hopes that diplomatic efforts could lead to a resolution to the conflict with Iran. The greenback was broadly higher against the yen, euro and pound.

Oil prices declined on hopes for diplomatic progress between the US and Iran. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian is expected to attend the United Nations General Assembly in New York this week, and US President Trump has said he would be open to meeting him. Energy producers posted losses, with Canadian Natural and Suncor shedding nearly 2% each. Miners were also mostly lower as gold prices eased, with WPM and Franco-Nevada losing about 1%. Meanwhile, yields eased as the halt in the oil rally cooled concerns over spreading inflationary pressures from energy costs, lending some support to credit-sensitive shares. Major banks rose, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, Scotiabank, and CIBC advancing nearly 1%, limiting losses in the index. Market participants await BoC Governor Macklem’s speech later in the day for clues on the bank’s future policy.

【Sources say Samsung HBM4 capacity may double next year】Industry sources say Samsung plans to raise outsourced glass-carrier cleaning throughput for its HBM process to 50,000 pieces/month next year from 20,000/month this year and 10,000/month last year. Glass carriers are temporary, reusable supports mounted beneath DRAM wafers to prevent warping or cracking during etch and drilling; actual carrier use depends on feed methods and process yield. A 2.5x rise versus this year suggests Samsung's HBM4-series output could at least double next year. Samsung is preparing to expand capacity for HBM4 and HBM4E, both using 12+-layer high-stack architectures.

【Russia preparing to extend diesel export ban beyond end-September】Russia is preparing to extend a diesel export ban beyond the end of September as Ukrainian attacks on its refineries continue, people familiar with the matter said. Moscow imposed the ban in July after strikes cut Russian refining throughput to a multi-year low; the measure, initially expected to last weeks, was extended monthly and to end-September, and officials are now considering another month-long or longer extension. Before the restrictions, Russian seaborne diesel supplied about 10% of the global market; removing that volume has compounded Middle East supply disruptions and pushed prices higher.

US Stocks Extend Increases — US stock indices rose for a third session on Monday as a pullback in oil prices softened Treasury yields and momentarily dampened a deteriorating macroeconomic backdrop. The S&P 500 and the Dow gained over 0.5%, while the Nasdaq 100 jumped 1%. Signs that the GCC extended diplomatic efforts for Tehran to lift their blockade on fuel tankers drove crude oil and product prices to ease further from their peaks this month, hence easing the recent pressure on Treasury securities. 【S&P: AI adoption will affect banks' credit ratings】S&P Global Ratings said banks' adoption of artificial intelligence will increasingly influence credit ratings. In a June survey of about 179 global financial institutions, respondents expect AI-related cost savings of up to 4% this year and 6–8% by 2028. Roughly 84% already use AI for support functions and automation, but fewer than one-third are using it to develop new products and services, citing regulatory and reputational risks. S&P said credit outcomes will hinge on the maturity of firms' AI strategies and the strength of their governance frameworks.

【Around 7,000 humanoid robots sold globally in 2025】The International Federation of Robotics (IFR) will publish on the 24th data showing roughly 7,000 humanoid robots were sold worldwide in 2025, covering only industrial and professional-service humanoids and excluding consumer, military and medical units. The IFR, which has compiled industrial and service-robot market data for almost 30 years, will for the first time report a separate humanoid-sales figure. Under the IFR definition a humanoid must be human-shaped (legs optional) and capable of autonomous operation in environments designed for humans. IFR secretary-general Susan Biller said many 2025 humanoids are not performing productive work but are being used by research institutes and companies to generate data to refine AI models. Refinitiv reported on the 21st.

【德銀：市場可能低估本輪全球加息幅度，但加息未必導致股市下跌】德意志銀行表示，投資者可能低估主要央行本輪加息週期的最終幅度。宏觀策略師Henry Allen指出，美聯儲、歐洲央行和日本央行過去兩週均已加息，而大宗商品價格上漲、金融環境仍偏寬鬆，意味著央行可能需要進一步收緊政策。布倫特原油目前約為每桶100美元，能源價格上漲尚未完全反映在通脹資料中；與此同時，標普500指數接近歷史高位，信用利差仍處低位。德銀指出，市場歷史上更容易低估加息幅度，2022年投資者最初預計美聯儲首年加息約200個基點，實際超過400個基點。不過，德銀認為加息並不必然導致股市下跌，並以1999年為例稱，當年美聯儲累計加息175個基點，標普500指數全年仍上漲近20%。

【智算中心上演交付競賽，算電協同迎來雙向基建大考】隨著大模型訓練規模擴大、推理應用加速落地，國內智算中心（AIDC）正在上演一場與時間賽跑的交付競賽，交付週期從“按年建設”轉向“按月交付”。對算力服務商而言，搶出的每一個月時間，意味著更早承接大模型客戶訂單、減少昂貴AI伺服器的閒置折舊。但極速交付背後，壓力也層層傳導。一方面考驗服務商預製化、模組化工程能力，機房內部供電、溫控以及園區負荷側電機系統整套架構面臨重構；另一方面，吉瓦（GW）級算力園區拔地而起，海量用電需求與新能源波動供電的矛盾浮現，算電協同不再是紙上談兵，而成為落到資源規劃、專案建設的現實命題。

### 2026-09-21 20:40:05  #MKT News

【Traders: Russia sharply boosts August diesel exports to Central Asia】Traders said Russia sharply increased diesel shipments to Central Asia in August despite a ban on diesel exports to most overseas markets, driven by a recovery in production. August exports to Kazakhstan, Kyrgyzstan, Mongolia and Tajikistan more than doubled from July, totaling over 370,000 tonnes: Mongolia about 215,000 tonnes (vs 124,000 in July); Kyrgyzstan rose from 4,400 tonnes to more than 72,000 tonnes; Tajikistan from 16,000 tonnes to over 56,000 tonnes; Kazakhstan imported roughly 28,000 tonnes in August after zero in July. Jan–Aug exports to Central Asia and Afghanistan were about 2.50 mln tonnes, slightly below 2.60 mln tonnes in the same period of 2025. Traders said Russian diesel output has recovered to roughly 170,000 tonnes/day but remains insufficient for a return to large-scale exports; domestic diesel supply fell to about 4.70 mln tonnes in August from 5.00 mln tonnes in July.

Treasury Yields Edge Down — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell 5bps to 4.95% on Monday, as traders started the new week with another decline in oil prices, amid hopes that diplomatic efforts could lead to a resolution to the conflict with Iran. Oil prices have now fallen for four consecutive sessions, easing some concerns over renewed inflationary pressures. Hopes of easing geopolitical tensions, supported by the summit between President Trump and President Xi as well as this week’s UN General Assembly, also helped improve investor sentiment. Meanwhile, Chicago Fed President Goolsbee said he remains open to the possibility that inflation will resume its decline towards the 2% target, but warned that interest rates may need to rise if that fails to materialise. Several other Fed officials are scheduled to speak this week, with markets closely watching their comments for further clues on the outlook for monetary policy. Traders currently expect the Fed to deliver another 25bps rate hike this year.

### 2026-09-21 20:31:29  #市場

【瑞銀預計白銀將繼續受益於黃金上漲，2027年9月目標看至80美元】瑞銀預計白銀將繼續緊隨黃金走勢，並受財政擔憂及美元長期購買力風險等因素支撐。策略師Dominic Schnider指出，金銀相關性已接近多年高位，近期白銀更多表現為“高貝塔版黃金”。瑞銀預計，白銀價格將在2026年12月升至每盎司70美元，2027年3月和6月升至75美元，並在2027年9月達到80美元，高於9月18日約66.50美元的現貨價格。該行認為，短期內更偏鷹派的貨幣政策仍可能帶來壓力，但較高金價、投資需求及工業需求將繼續提供支撐。工業端方面，太陽能行業雖在高價下減少白銀使用，但資料中心、AI基礎設施、電網投資和電動車需求有望部分抵消這一影響。與此同時，白銀多數作為鉛、鋅、銅和黃金開採的副產品，供應難以快速響應價格上漲。瑞銀認為，中期風險仍偏向銀價上行。

Brent Crude Drops for 4th Day — Brent crude fell to around $101.2 a barrel on Monday, extending losses for a fourth session as traders focused on diplomatic efforts to de-escalate the US-Iran conflict and signs that oil and LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz remain resilient. President Donald Trump said he would probably be open to meeting Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian during this week’s UN General Assembly in New York. Trump may also meet with other Persian Gulf leaders, while he is scheduled to hold a summit with Chinese President Xi Jinping. Meanwhile, US Central Command chief Admiral Brad Cooper said crude and LNG flows through Hormuz have reached a six-month high, with the main transit lanes clear of mines. Middle East oil flows averaged 17.1 million barrels per day over the past 10 days, despite Saudi Arabia’s East-West pipeline remaining disrupted after recent attacks.

【雷蒙德·詹姆斯：全球穀物供應趨緊，2026/27年度玉米缺口料創33年來最大】雷蒙德·詹姆斯表示，受產量下降、需求強勁以及黑海出口能力受限影響，2026年全球穀物市場明顯收緊。Zaner Ag Hedge首席市場分析師Karen Braun的判斷稱，2026/27年度全球玉米消費量預計將超過產量約2900萬噸，為33年來最大的絕對供需缺口。美國玉米庫存消費比已由年內早些時候的12.1%降至9.7%，跌破10%，而晚夏作物評級下滑及高溫天氣仍令最終產量面臨進一步下調風險。與此同時，主要出口國玉米和小麥合計產量預計同比減少8400萬噸。供需收緊已推動價格走高，自5月1日以來，玉米和小麥期貨均上漲約8%，大豆上漲約12%。

【香港交易所擬進一步最佳化上市規則，須股東批准的主要交易門檻由25%提高至50%】香港交易及結算所有限公司（以下簡稱香港交易所）全資附屬公司香港聯合交易所有限公司（以下簡稱香港聯交所）刊發《諮詢檔案》，就一系列建議徵詢市場意見。各項建議旨在最佳化上市發行人有關公司交易的監管規定，從而提升香港上市機制的競爭力。諮詢期為期10周，至2026年11月30日結束。《諮詢檔案》顯示，香港聯交所建議，須股東批准的主要交易門檻由25%提高至50%；但涉及提供財務資助、證券或其他投資活動的交易除外，仍維持在25%。同時，取消非常重大出售事項及非常重大收購事項分類。

【上海市政府常務會議傳達學習習近平總書記關於發展先進製造業的重要指示精神，研究做好三季度經濟社會發展各項工作】上海市委副書記、代市長朱忠明今天（9月21日）主持召開市政府常務會議，傳達學習習近平總書記關於發展先進製造業的重要指示精神和全國先進製造業大會部署；研究做好三季度經濟社會發展各項工作。會議指出，要切實把思想和行動統一到習近平總書記重要指示精神上來，按照李強總理的講話要求，在市委堅強領導下，把發展先進製造業作為一項重要戰略任務，持續用力、久久為功，奮力在推動先進製造業發展中走在前列、勇為尖兵。要錨定目標，夯實發展根基，努力形成傳統產業穩健託底、新興產業有力支撐、未來產業蓄勢賦能的梯次發展格局。要堅持以科技創新推動產業創新，集中力量攻堅關鍵核心技術，提升產業發展“含新量”，加快形成“產業出題、科技答題、市場閱卷”的良性迴圈。要以智慧化為牽引、以綠色化為導向、以融合化為支撐，加快轉型升級，發展新一代智慧製造，構建綠色製造體系，促進先進製造業與現代服務業、數字技術與實體經濟深度融合，持續提升上海先進製造業的核心競爭力。（上海釋出）

### 2026-09-21 20:04:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Construction Output #Importance 2

India Infrastructure Output Slows — Infrastructure output in India rose by 4.8% from the previous year in August of 2026, easing from the downwardly revised 5% growth rate in the earlier month, but remaining above the lower rates following the initial response from the war in the Middle East. Growth was carried by construction materials, with output remaining high for cement (12.5% vs 12.7% in July), steel (3.4% vs 1.9%), and iron ore (5.5% vs 29.5%). In turn, energy remained under pressure as the war triggered a surge in fuel prices and costs that Indian manufacturers face. Output fell for crude oil (-3.6% vs -5.3%), natural gas (-4.9% vs -3%), and slowed for refinery products (2.6% vs 3.0%). Soaring LNG prices faced by Asian importers also hampered production of fertilizers (-12.4% vs -8%).

### 2026-09-21 19:57:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 3

US Futures Extend Rebound — US equity futures were firmly higher on Monday, rebounding from losses last week as a pullback in oil prices softened Treasury yields and momentarily dampened a deteriorating macroeconomic backdrop. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Dow rose around 0.6% and Nasdaq 100 gained up to 1%. Signs that the GCC extended diplomatic efforts for Tehran to lift their blockade on fuel tankers drove crude oil and product prices to ease further from their peaks this month, hence easing the recent pressure on Treasury securities. Lower credit costs improved the outlook for AI hyperscalers to continue issuing record levels of debt, supporting chip producers and AI infrastructure stocks. The sector was also impacted by debates on risks around AI safety. Intel, AMD, Seagate, Marvell, and Western Digital rose between 3% and 5%. Meanwhile, Meta added more than 2.5% on positive responses to its Muse AI agent. Banks also edged higher on lower borrowing costs.

【德銀維持大眾汽車“買入”評級，稱剔除特殊專案後2026年利潤率仍接近4%】德意志銀行研究稱，大眾汽車將2026年營業利潤率指引下調至最高1%，主要受約100億歐元特殊專案拖累，若剔除這些影響，基礎營業利潤率約為4%，與此前市場4.1%的預期基本一致。相關特殊專案包括超過60億歐元的保時捷相關非現金減值，以及超過20億歐元與提前退休計劃和出售奧斯納布呂克工廠有關的減值。受此影響，德銀將大眾2026年報告口徑營業利潤預測由145.5億歐元大幅下調至29.5億歐元，但調整後營業利潤預測僅由156.5億歐元下調至125.5億歐元，對應調整後營業利潤率由4.8%降至4%。德銀認為，大眾當前重組成本較高，未來數月仍可能出現額外費用。不過，公司仍維持30億至60億歐元汽車業務淨現金流及320億至340億歐元淨流動性指引，德銀預計現金流有望接近區間上端，並維持115歐元目標價和“買入”評級。

### 2026-09-21 19:45:48  #市場 #國際

【加密貨幣集體飆漲，14萬人爆倉】9月21日下午，加密貨幣集體大漲，比特幣直線拉昇，一度站上85000美元/枚。截至發稿，比特幣報84915.1美元/枚，過去24小時內漲5.42%，創1月底以來新高。此外，以太坊站上2700美元/枚，SOL、XRP、狗狗幣等漲幅均超6%。Coinglass資料顯示，過去24小時加密貨幣市場共有13.6萬人爆倉，爆倉總金額為7.5億美元，其中多單爆倉金額為1億美元，空單爆倉金額為6.5億美元。訊息面上，當地時間9月17日，美國證券交易委員會（SEC）釋出了一項名為“創新豁免”（Innovation Exemption）的規則，允許符合特定條件的交易平臺在美國提供代幣化股票交易，並給予相關平臺為期5年的多項監管豁免。SEC表示，此舉旨在允許市場參與者在受控環境中探索鏈上股票交易，同時為監管機構制定長期規則積累經驗。（第一財經）

【摩根大通執行長戴蒙將赴印度 華爾街加碼爭奪印度投行業務】摩根大通執行長傑米·戴蒙本週將前往印度。隨著這家華爾街巨頭加大對印度市場的拓展力度，該行預計當地交易活動將迎來進一步繁榮。戴蒙計劃出席摩根大通在孟買舉辦的年度投資會議，並將在會上與印度大型企業集團的高管會面。近年來，戴蒙一直定期訪問印度。摩根大通也在當地不斷擴充投資銀行、支付解決方案等業務領域的團隊。去年，摩根大通在印度股票發行市場排名第一，在這個全球增長最快的經濟體之一、也是最活躍的融資市場之一拔得頭籌。摩根大通高管預計，這股熱潮還將持續。“我們看到跨境併購、創新經濟、製造業、醫療保健、消費、金融服務和科技等領域存在重大機遇，”摩根大通印度高階國家主管Rahul Badhwar在戴蒙訪問前透過電子郵件表示。“隨著印度企業繼續向全球擴張，跨國公司進一步擴大在印度的業務，對戰略諮詢、融資和資本市場專業服務的需求只會不斷增加。”

### 2026-09-21 19:24:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Switzerland #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

Swiss Bond Yield Falls to One-Week Low — The yield on the Swiss 10-year government bond fell below 0.52%, reaching its lowest level in over a week, as declining global oil prices eased concerns over inflationary pressures. On the monetary policy front, the Swiss National Bank is set to announce its policy rate decision on Thursday and is widely expected to leave the rate unchanged at 0%. A Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all respondents expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% through the end of the year. Markets are pricing in the first rate hike for June 2027, while most economists expect the first increase in early 2028. Meanwhile, the State Secretariat for Economic Affairs raised its forecast for economic growth in 2026 to 1.7%, as the impact of higher energy prices remains limited, up from its June projection of 0.9% and broadly in line with the OECD’s latest forecast of 2%.

### 2026-09-21 18:20:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Latvia Producer Inflation at 3-Month Low — Producer prices in Latvia increased 2.6% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a revised 3.3% in July and marking the lowest reading since May. Cost growth moderated for mining and quarrying (4.9% vs 7.8% in July), while it eased sharply for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (1.0% vs 8.0%). Meanwhile, price growth accelerated in manufacturing (2.5% vs 1.9%), driven by faster increases in beverages (3.6% vs 3.5%), wood and products of wood and cork, except furniture, and manufacture of articles of straw and plaiting materials (5.4% vs 4.0%), paper and paper products (2.6% vs 0.4%), chemicals and chemical products (8.2% vs 7.4%), rubber and plastic products (6.2% vs 3.9%), and fabricated metal products, except machinery and equipment (3.5% vs 2.4%), while prices also rose for water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities (9.1% vs 8.7%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.5% in August, reversing a revised 0.1% fall in July.

### 2026-09-21 18:19:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lebanon #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Lebanon Inflation Rate Rises in August — The annual inflation rate in Lebanon rose to 16.7% in August 2026 from a five-month low of 15.7% in the previous month. Prices increased at a faster pace in housing and utilities (17.9% vs 14.5% in July), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (9.6% vs 6.8%), transportation (31.7% vs 27.6%), recreation, amusement and culture (45.7% vs 43.2%), health (7.4% vs 7.3%), and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (7.1% vs 7.0%), while remaining unchanged in education (at 35.72%). Meanwhile, inflation eased in food and non-alcoholic beverages (11.7% vs 14.5%), clothing and footwear (1.2% vs 1.7%), restaurants and hotels (14.3% vs 14.6%), and miscellaneous goods and services (15.7% vs 16.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased 1.4%, rebounding from a 0.03% decline in July.

### 2026-09-21 18:11:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Luxembourg Unemployment Rises to Highest Since 2020 — Luxembourg’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate rose to 6.5% in August 2026, the highest level since July 2020, up from 6.3% in the previous month. The number of unemployed persons increased by 464 over the month to 20,900, with the increase particularly pronounced among highly qualified job seekers (19%). By age group, the largest increase was among those aged 30 to 44 (13.9%), followed by those aged 16 to 29 (13.5%). Total employment also rose by 139 to 302,888, while the labor force population increased by 602 to 323,787. Regarding cross-border employment, the number of outgoing resident workers increased by 26 to 15,544, while the number of incoming cross-border workers rose by 227 to 235,753. In August 2025, the seasonally adjusted unemployment rate stood at 5.9%.

French Bond Yields Retreat as Oil Falls, Fiscal Concerns Mount — French 10-year OAT yield fell below 4.5%, retreating from last week's 18-year highs as oil prices continued to fall amid tentative signs of easing geopolitical tensions and stronger regional supply. Meanwhile, concerns over the country’s finances intensified after Scope Ratings downgraded the country to A+ from AA-, citing rising debt, persistent deficits and limited progress on structural reforms. Morningstar DBRS also shifted its outlook on France’s AA rating to negative. The warnings come as the government struggles to rein in spending, with the budget deficit now expected to widen to 5.4% of GDP this year, from 5.1% in 2025. French bond spreads have already widened sharply, with the 10-year OAT-Bund premium briefly exceeding 100 basis points for the first time in 14 years. Political uncertainty ahead of next year’s presidential election is further complicating efforts to secure support for the government’s planned fiscal consolidation.

Slovenia Producer Inflation Hits Over 3-Year High — Slovenia’s producer prices rose 3.5% year-on-year in August 2026, up from 3.4% in the previous month and marking the highest level since July 2023. Prices increased for manufacturing (3.7% vs 3.5% in July), particularly for textiles, wearing apparel, paper and paper products, coke and refined petroleum products, chemicals and chemical products, and basic metals. Price growth also accelerated for mining and quarrying (8.4% vs 7.4%). In contrast, prices moderated for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (1.9% vs 2.1%), while deflation in water supply remained unchanged at 4.0%. Among the main industrial groups, prices rose for energy (2.6% vs 2.0%), and intermediate goods (5.8% vs 5.1%). Meanwhile, costs eased for capital goods (2.4% vs 2.8%), and consumer goods (1.0% vs 1.4%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.3% in August, easing from 0.7% in July and marking the lowest reading since March.

### 2026-09-21 16:49:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Current Account #Importance 1

Hong Kong Current Account Surplus Widens in Q2 — Hong Kong’s current account surplus widened to HKD 96.0 billion in the second quarter of 2026, from HKD 93.1 billion in the same period a year earlier. The increase was mainly driven by a higher net primary income inflow, which rose to HKD 123.7 billion from HKD 107.0 billion, and a larger services surplus, which increased to HKD 38.5 billion from HKD 30.9 billion. Primary income inflows rose to HKD 664.6 billion from HKD 624.6 billion, while outflows increased to HKD 540.9 billion from HKD 517.6 billion. These improvements were partly offset by a wider goods deficit, which increased to HKD 59.1 billion from HKD 38.3 billion, and a larger secondary income deficit of HKD 7.0 billion from HKD 6.4 billion.

### 2026-09-21 16:29:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Current Account #Importance 1

Greece Current Account Surplus Narrows in July — Greece’s current account surplus narrowed to EUR 0.22 billion in July 2026, from EUR 0.67 billion in the same month last year. The goods deficit rose to EUR 3.15 billion from EUR 2.92 billion a year earlier, as imports (EUR 8.12 billion) grew more than exports (EUR 4.97 billion) in absolute terms. At the same time, the primary income gap widened to EUR 0.68 billion from EUR 0.57 billion, reflecting higher net interest, dividend, and profit payments. The secondary income deficit also increased to EUR 0.60 billion from EUR 0.24 billion a year earlier, owing to higher net payments mainly in the general government sector. Meanwhile, the services surplus edged up to EUR 4.65 billion from EUR 4.39 billion, mainly attributed to an increase in the travel balance.

Poland Corporate Wage Growth Less Than Expected — Poland’s corporate sector wages rose 5.6% year-on-year to PLN 9,259.54 in August 2026, slowing from 6.8% in July and coming below market expectations of 6.6%. European Stocks Rise on Monday — European stocks opened the week higher, with the STOXX 50 gaining 1.1% and the STOXX 600 rising 0.7%, as oil prices extended their declines for a fourth consecutive session, providing some relief on inflationary pressures. Sentiment was also supported by expectations surrounding this week’s summit between US President Trump and Chinese President Xi Jinping, with the US describing its talks with China ahead of the meeting as “very successful”. Investors were also digesting the results of state elections in Germany. The CDU recorded a very low vote share, adding further pressure on Chancellor Merz. The far-right AfD is leading in Mecklenburg-Western Pomerania while in Berlin, the Left emerged as the leading party.Technology stocks were among the strongest performers, with ASML Holding, Infineon and STMicroelectronics each gaining around 2.8%. Societe Generale rose approximately 3.6%, making it one of the top performers on the STOXX 600, after the bank raised its profitability target.

India 10Y Yield Steady as RBI Sale Looms — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec hovered around 7.0%, trading in a tight range as lower oil prices offered some support, while the RBI’s liquidity-draining bond sale, elevated US Treasury yields and firmer rate hike expectations capped gains. Brent crude fell 2.3% to around $101.50 a barrel, easing inflation concerns, although traders said a sustained move below $100 would be needed for the 10-year yield to decline meaningfully toward 7%. Meanwhile, the RBI is set to sell INR 250 billion of bonds through an open market operation, its second such sale in a series of measures to withdraw excess liquidity from the banking system. Expectations of a 25-basis-point RBI rate hike on October 7 have also strengthened following the Federal Reserve’s recent move, while the US 10-year Treasury yield remained near 5% after the Fed signalled another hike before year-end.

### 2026-09-21 15:31:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Georgia Trade Deficit Smallest in 7 Months — Georgia’s trade deficit narrowed to USD 809.9 million in August 2026 from USD 894.4 million a year earlier, marking the smallest trade shortfall since January. Outbound shipments climbed 24.8% year-on-year to USD 752.4 million, while imports increased at a much slower pace of 4.3% to a four-month low of USD 1.56 billion. In the first eight months of 2026, exports surged 22.1% to USD 5.42 billion, supported by strong gains in petroleum and petroleum oils (1,067.7%) and precious metal ores and concentrates (68.6%). Export growth was particularly strong to China (177.3%) and Russia (4.1%). Meanwhile, imports rose 3.4% to USD 12.5 billion during the January–August period, largely driven by higher purchases of petroleum and petroleum oils (37.4%), despite a decline in motor car imports (-12.5%). Imports recorded the largest increases from Russia (24.9%) and China (22.1%). Georgia’s trade deficit for the January–August period reached USD 7.08 billion.

【小摩：三星電子第三季預覽儲存器業務保持強勁，維持“增持”評級】摩根大通發表研報指，三星電子第三季業績預覽顯示，儲存器業務營運保持強勁，惟短期受匯兌逆風影響。該行維持“增持”評級及目標價40萬韓元，認為中期風險回報有利，建議投資者吸納。該行上調HBM業務假設，預期三星電子HBM價值市佔由2025年的20%升至2026年及2027年的34%及39%，並預測2027年財政年度HBM混合平均售價同比升64%。受韓元走強影響，該行將2026年及2027年調整後每股盈利預測分別下調4%及4.6%，至分別48,788韓元及68,995韓元。考慮外匯變動，該行下調今年第三及第四季經營溢利預測介乎7萬億至9萬億韓元，至介乎102萬億至109萬億韓元，低於市場預測介乎109萬億至121萬億韓元。並輕微下調明後兩年每股盈測，反映韓元轉強，以美元計預測上調，源於HBM業務量、定價及利潤率預測獲上調。該行認為積極的股東回報對股價順利回升屬必要，並視第三季業績電話會議上的中期更新為下一個關鍵觀察點。

### 2026-09-21 15:20:08  #MKT News #03396.HK #Impact bullish

【CITIGROUP: Legend Holdings' potential sale of BIL could narrow NAV discount by up to 75%; Buy】CITIGROUP says Legend Holdings (03396.HK) is reportedly seeking to sell about 90% of Luxembourg private bank Banque Internationale à Luxembourg (BIL), a unit Citi estimates represents roughly 9% of Legend’s NAV. Reported preliminary bids, due around end-September, imply a 100% BIL value of at least €2.5bn (~HKD22.5bn), about 3% above Citi’s latest valuation of ~HKD21.8bn (0.9x book at end-1H2026). BIL has been a stable profit contributor, but Citi expects its share of Legend’s net profit to fall to 10–13% in FY2027–28 as Lenovo Group (00992.HK) drives stronger earnings growth. Citi views a BIL sale as a step toward divesting non-core assets and refocusing Legend as a tech holding; if executed, Citi estimates it could cut Legend’s NAV discount by as much as ~75%. Citi maintains a Buy rating on Legend Holdings.

### 2026-09-21 15:17:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Georgia Producer Inflation Accelerates in August — Producer prices in Georgia rose 5.8% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from 5.1% in the previous month. The acceleration reflected faster price growth in mining and quarrying (20.1% vs 14.4% in July). Moreover, prices for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply increased (2.6% vs -1.7%), while inflation for water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation services accelerated (6.3% vs 5.7%). Meanwhile, manufacturing inflation eased slightly (5.1% vs 5.2%). Within manufacturing, price growth slowed for chemicals and chemical products (13.3% vs 15.4%) and basic metals (10.1% vs 11.0%), while food product inflation also eased (5.7% vs 6.6%), whereas coke and refined petroleum products accelerated (33.0% vs 6.0%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.9% in August, following a 0.7% decline in July.

### 2026-09-21 15:16:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Moldova Industrial Output Growth Slows in July — Industrial production in Moldova rose by 7.1% year-on-year in July 2026, easing from a three-month high of 10.6% in the previous month. Output growth slowed in manufacturing (6.2% vs 10.2% in June), weighed down by notable decreases in food products (11.1% vs 18.4%), textiles (24.7% vs 40.1%), computers, electronics, and optical products (14.3% vs 36.2%), and metal construction and metal products (15.2% vs 19.4%). Moreover, activity also moderated in the electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply sector (18.0% vs 19.4%). On the other hand, production growth accelerated in mining and quarrying (20.5% vs 10.3%). On a monthly basis, industrial output increased by 7.6% in July, rebounding from a 0.9% decline in the preceding period. In the January–July period, industrial production rose by 7.7%.

### 2026-09-21 15:14:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Capacity Utilization #Importance 1

Turkey Factory Capacity Usage Rises to 3-Month High — The manufacturing industry in Turkey operated at 74.2% of its production capacity in September 2026, marking the highest rate since June and up from a five-month low of 73.5% in the previous month. The increase was driven mainly by investment goods, where capacity utilization jumped to 73.1% from 69.7%, pointing to stronger activity in capital-intensive industries. Utilization also increased for non-durable consumer goods, rising to 72.5% from 72.2%, while food and beverages edged up to 71.6% from 71.4%. On the other hand, durable consumer goods utilization fell to 66.4% from 68.2%, while overall consumer goods utilization eased to 71.4% from 71.6%. Intermediate goods utilization also declined slightly to 74.5% from 74.7%, although it remained the highest among the major categories.

【標普預計韓國未來10年資料中心投資規模將達約9000億美元】標準普爾全球（S&P Global）警告稱，隨著韓國企業參與作為“三大超級專案”一部分推進的大規模資料中心建設，其投資負擔預計將大幅增加，並可能進一步導致企業信用評級承壓。標普21日在一份報告中表示，預計未來10年韓國資料中心投資規模將達到約9000億美元（約合1200萬億韓元），電信運營商等參與企業將大幅擴張資料中心業務。但與此同時，企業投資負擔將迅速增加，可能對其信用狀況造成壓力。標普指出，為將對信用狀況的影響降至最低，韓國企業很可能像海外資料中心建設專案一樣，採用資產證券化和專案融資（PF）等結構，但資本支出的顯著增加仍不可避免。據市場調查機構451 Research統計，目前韓國在亞太地區資料中心市場中的佔比僅為2.5%，規模相對較小。

### 2026-09-21 15:08:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Turkey Manufacturing Confidence Falls to 5-Month Low — Turkey’s manufacturing coincidence index fell to 102 in September 2026 from 102.8 in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since April. The decline was driven by weaker production and export expectations, with output over the next three months falling to 115.6 from 121.1 in August, while export orders for the coming three months dropped to 111.9 from 118.3. Expectations for fixed investment expenditure also eased to 104.9 from 105.5, and total orders over the past three months edged down to 104.2 from 104.4. However, firms reported stronger current orders, rising to 87.8 from 84.8, while employment expectations increased to 104.4 from 103.3. Inventories of finished goods fell to 96.9 from 98.1, indicating lower stock levels, while expectations for the general business situation improved to 90.4 from 86.7.

### 2026-09-21 14:53:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

UK Natgas Prices Fall as Traders Monitor Middle East Diplomacy — UK natural gas prices dropped to 193 pence per therm on Monday after surging in the previous session, as traders monitored diplomatic efforts to end the Middle East conflict. President Donald Trump said he would probably be open to meeting Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian, who is expected to attend the UN General Assembly later this week, though Tehran has yet to respond to the remarks. Any progress toward de-escalation could ease concerns over prolonged LNG supply disruptions. However, supply risks remain elevated, with the conflict continuing to restrict LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz. European gas storage facilities were around 68.5% full, still below the seasonal average. The situation is further complicated by intense competition for LNG with Asia and ongoing maintenance work on Norway’s gas infrastructure. With the heating season approaching, the market remains highly sensitive to extended supply disruptions, increasing the risk of further price volatility.

【海內外算力產業延續景氣共振，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）三連漲】9月21日，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）繼續活躍，截至發稿漲幅0.85%，成交額1.61億元。資金流向方面，上週五獲得1900萬元淨申購，最新規模3.3億元。中信建投證券認為，算力行情演繹正從核心資產抱團沿缺貨漲價、新需求挖掘與產能擠佔三重邏輯，向全產業鏈景氣擴散轉變，後續超額收益將更多來源於AI算力產業鏈中卡位清晰、景氣收益明確且尚未充分定價的細分環節。招商證券指出，華為超節點依託靈衢互聯與NPO技術，將算力競爭由單卡轉向系統級，當前已進入規模化商用階段，帶動高速互聯、光引擎、液冷等產業鏈增量。建議關注供應鏈核心標的，把握國產算力叢集放量機遇。興業證券表示，國內華為宣佈950超節點叢集后續開啟商用，海外黃仁勳預計2027年晶片出貨量同比翻倍，Nebius宣佈上調按需算力租賃價格，海內外算力產業延續景氣共振。資料顯示，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）跟蹤創業板算力基礎設施指數（970083），聚焦資料中心（AIDC）建設與運營、算力租賃、液冷伺服器等算力核心環節。該指數“資料中心（AIDC）”概念含量達91.03%，為全市場最高，算力租賃概念含量38.84%，成分股覆蓋協創資料、潤澤科技、光環新網等算力基建核心標的。看好算力的投資者，請認準創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）。

### 2026-09-21 14:38:40  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美債收益率飆升之際 新興市場投資者更青睞本幣債】隨著美國國債收益率飆升削弱美元計價新興市場債券的吸引力，新興市場投資者目前仍傾向於持有本幣主權債券。這一策略背後的驅動力在於具有吸引力的估值，以及透過套息交易獲取收益的潛力，即投資者借入低收益貨幣並投資於高收益資產。這種偏好體現在資金流向、持倉狀況和相對錶現上。自6月底以來，彭博新興市場本幣債券指數已跑贏美元計價債券指數超過3個百分點，有望錄得自2022年以來最大的季度領先幅度。基金經理也在利用這種分化行情。美國銀行對管理資產規模合計達4,440億美元的38位全球固定收益基金經理進行的調查顯示，其中84%的受訪者相對於硬通貨債券超配新興市場本幣債，而8月份這一比例僅為38%。調查於9月4日至9日進行。不過，在美聯儲加息並暗示可能進一步收緊政策後，這一投資策略仍可能面臨考驗。若美元重新走強，可能促使投資者撤離本幣債等新興市場資產。

【機構：受益於北美市場需求持續上調，Enterprise SSD第四季價格預估將持續上行】根據TrendForce集邦諮詢研究顯示，過往兩週以來美系雲端廠商對Enterprise SSD（企業級SSD）的需求上調，主要原因在於各大CSP正在加速擴大Enterprise SSD的部署規模與應用場景。值得注意的是，這波需求不僅集中於高效能TLC產品，單位容量成本更低、效益更高的QLC產品更是成長動能的主要來源。從近期拉貨動能觀察，第四季 Enterprise SSD訂單總量有望突破第三季的高點，呈現持續向上攀升的強勁態勢。這也使得原廠在企業級市場的產能配置與議價主導權顯著提升，進一步鞏固Enterprise SSD價格維持上行軌道的基礎。

US 10-Year Yield Eases Ahead of Fed Remarks — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note fell by about 3 basis points to 4.97% on Monday as investors awaited several appearances by Federal Reserve officials this week for further clues on the monetary policy outlook. On Sunday, Minneapolis Fed President Neel Kashkari said inflation remains too high and that price pressures have expanded beyond the oil-price shock linked to the Iran war. Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee is scheduled to speak later Monday, followed by New York Fed President John Williams on Tuesday. Global bond yields also eased as oil prices fell for a fourth consecutive session, helping reduce inflation concerns. Last week, the benchmark yield surged to a 19-year high as the Fed raised interest rates and signaled at least one more hike this year, while Chair Warsh reiterated the central bank’s commitment to containing inflation.

### 2026-09-21 14:08:39  #其他

【國家新聞出版署有關負責同志就《出版業發展“十五五”規劃》答記者問】問：請介紹規劃的結構和主要任務。
答：規劃共10章，分3個板塊。第一板塊即第一章，為總體要求，主要對“十五五”時期出版業發展形勢進行研判，明確“十五五”時期出版業發展的指導思想、基本原則和主要目標。第二板塊包括第二章至第九章，圍繞著力做好主題出版、持續擴大出版優質供給、深化全民閱讀工作、加快出版深度融合發展、推進報業系統性變革、推動印刷發行業創新發展、加強出版國際傳播、建設高水平出版人才隊伍等8個方面，提出“十五五”時期需要著力推進實施的重點任務。相關任務舉措經過深入分析論證，聚焦出版業重點領域和關鍵環節，有利於鞏固拓展優勢、突破瓶頸堵點、補強短板弱項、提高質量效益，支撐出版業“十五五”時期發展目標順利實現。第三板塊即第十章，為保障與實施，就完善保障措施、推動規劃落地見效提出要求。
問：請介紹規劃的主要亮點。
答：規劃引領推動未來五年出版業繁榮發展，突出加強內容建設、強化閱讀引領、深化融合發展等舉措。一是加強內容建設，擴大優質供給。規劃提出改進出版內容生產服務、引導、組織工作機制，提高出版原創能力和內容質量，做好主題出版和各類精品出版，透過一系列重點專案，引導推出彰顯時代主流價值、反映社會生動實踐的重點出版物，弘揚中華文化、傳播先進知識、滋養人民心靈，無愧於時代、能夠傳諸後世的經典作品。二是強化閱讀引領，涵育閱讀風尚。規劃強調發揮全民閱讀在文化強國建設中的重要作用，貫徹執行《全民閱讀促進條例》，統籌優質內容供給、閱讀設施建設、閱讀服務創新、重點群體閱讀權益保障和閱讀消費促進，推動全民閱讀融入文明建立和城鄉基層文化建設。三是加快融合重塑，培育發展動能。規劃將深度融合發展作為出版單位加快數智化賦能、資訊化轉型的重要路徑，以出版融合發展工程為牽引，推動重塑組織架構、業務流程、產品樣態、傳播體系、激勵機制等，統籌推進報業、印刷、發行等領域數智化轉型，促進內容、技術、平臺、業態深度融合，構建形成內容增值、融合共生、開放創新的產業發展新生態。

### 2026-09-21 14:06:04  #公司 #國際

【中國駐清邁總領館釋出中秋國慶假期來泰旅遊注意事項】中秋國慶假期將至，為了您和家人的泰國之旅平安順利，中國駐泰國使領館溫馨提示如下：

一、做好行前準備。提前確認護照有效期不少於6個月且無破損，備妥往返或前往第三國（地區）機票、酒店或住宿證明及足額資金。入境泰國前按規定填報泰國電子入境卡，務必透過泰國移民局官方網站免費辦理，謹防假冒網站和收費代辦騙局。建議提前購買境外旅遊保險。

二、依法依規入境。中國公民可根據中泰免籤協定赴泰旅遊、探親等，單次停留不超過30日。免籤不等於無條件入境，泰方移民官員有權依法查驗入境目的及相關材料。赴泰國工作、學習、新聞報導等須事先辦妥相應簽證，切勿逾期居留或從事與入境事由不符的活動。近期泰方加強對泰緬邊境地區出入人員管理，請謹慎前往達府、湄索等相關地區。

三、關注天氣和自然災害風險。泰國部分地區近期仍可能出現強降雨、雷暴等天氣，山區存在山洪、滑坡等風險，南部海域可能出現較大風浪。出行前請密切關注天氣預報和當地預警資訊，合理調整行程，遠離危險區域。

四、提高旅遊安全意識。請妥善保管護照、手機、錢包等證件財物，選擇正規交通工具和旅行服務機構，切勿無證駕駛摩托車或汽車，謹慎參加叢林飛越、洞穴探險、浮潛等高風險專案，量力而行並聽從專業人員指揮，全程正確使用安全裝置。海灘出現紅旗時切勿下海，嚴格遵守酒店泳池開放時間。

五、文明出行，理性維權。尊重泰國法律法規、宗教信仰、風俗習慣和文化傳統，遵守景區、場館等場所管理規定，文明有序出行。切勿參與賭博、吸毒等違法活動，警惕“高薪招聘”“包機票、包食宿”等詐騙陷阱。與旅行社、航空公司等發生糾紛時，請保持冷靜，注意儲存證據，透過協商、投訴或法律途徑理性維權。

### 2026-09-21 13:53:29  #MKT News

【Bank of Korea launches 24-hour offshore won settlement pilot】The Bank of Korea said on Monday it has launched a pilot for a 24-hour offshore won settlement network to support efforts to lower foreign investor access barriers. The BOK Won International Wire Network began trial operation at 09:00 with four domestic banks—Kookmin Bank, Woori Bank, Hana Bank and Shinhan Bank—as partners. Full implementation is planned for January, after which overseas banks will join. The network will run 24 hours from 09:00 each business day to 09:00 the following day, but will be closed on weekends and public holidays. The system will let offshore investors use accounts opened under RFI-K (registered foreign institutions for won business) in their home jurisdictions to transact won with other foreign investors.

NZX 50 Erases Morning Losses, Ends 0.6% Higher — The NZX 50 climbed 82 points, or 0.6%, to close at 13,821 on Monday, erasing losses from the morning and previous session, mainly buoyed by gains in consumer staples, materials, and communication services. The broader index hit its highest level since September 7, tracking a rise in US stock futures after the Dow Jones recorded a third straight losing week. Meanwhile, China held interest rates for a 16th month ahead of the Trump-Xi summit on September 24. However, traders continued to monitor oil prices amid efforts to ease tensions in the Middle East. Last week's economic data limited the gain, as New Zealand’s GDP grew 0.1% quarter-on-quarter in Q2, down from 0.2% previously, marking the weakest growth in a year, as higher fuel costs weighed on businesses and consumers. Among notable gainers were Vulcan Steel (5.8%), A2 Milk (4.5%), Skellerup Holdings (3.5%), Mainfreight (2.5%), Delegat Group 2.2%), Freightways Group (1.2%), ANZ Group (0.9%), and Fisher & Paykel (0.9%).

### 2026-09-21 13:31:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Estonia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Estonia Producer Inflation Slows in August — Estonia’s producer prices rose by 3.3% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from 3.5% in the previous month. Price growth slowed in mining and quarrying (3.4% vs 4.7% in July), while it declined in electricity, gas, steam, and air conditioning, falling sharply by 13.8% from 8.4% previously. Meanwhile, producer prices in manufacturing accelerated to 4.8% from 3.5%, driven mainly by higher prices for beverages, leather and related products, wood and wood products, paper and paper products, coke and refined petroleum products, chemicals and chemical products, basic pharmaceutical products and pharmaceutical preparations, other non-metallic mineral products, fabricated metal products, except machinery and equipment, and machinery and equipment n.e.c. Costs in water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities remained unchanged at 3.4%. On a monthly basis, producer prices advanced 1.4%, rebounding from a 0.6% drop in the previous month.

### 2026-09-21 13:08:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Singapore Q2 Jobless Rate Revised Lower — Singapore’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate stood at 1.9% in Q2 2026, slightly below the preliminary estimate of 2.0% and the lowest since Q4 2024, amid stronger economic growth. Yet labour conditions stayed uneven. Layoffs rose to 4,620, the highest since Q4 2020, up from 3,830 in Q1, led by outward-oriented sectors including manufacturing, communications and finance, with restructuring as the main driver. The retrenchment rate increased to 2.0 per 1,000 employees from 1.6 in Q1. Total employment growth accelerated (11,400 vs 9,400 in Q1), mainly driven by non-resident hires as resident gains slowed. Re-entry outcomes dipped, with 54.9% of retrenched residents returning to work versus 60.7% prior. Job vacancies fell (68,600 vs 73,300) and 76,200 in Q2 2025, though still outnumbered unemployed, at 1.48 per job seeker. Forward indicators were mixed: more firms plan to hire, but wage-growth intentions eased, both below February levels, due to caution over hiring and wage increases.

Iron Ore Rises for Fourth Straight Session — Iron ore futures climbed above CNY 715 per ton on Monday, extending gains for a fourth consecutive session on expectations that Chinese steel mills will increase raw material purchases ahead of the long Golden Week holiday in early October. The demand outlook also improved after several Chinese steelmakers pledged to cut production in an effort to reduce steel inventories as profit margins shrink rapidly. Profitability across China’s steel industry deteriorated sharply, weighed down by elevated production costs and weak demand for steel. However, expectations that major coking coal mines will resume operations in September and October following a fatal mine accident in May improved the supply outlook for a key steelmaking ingredient. Meanwhile, industry data showed that iron ore inventories at 35 major Chinese ports increased by 840,000 tons week-on-week to 144.33 million tons as of September 18, reversing an earlier destocking trend.

Sensex Opens Week Higher — India’s BSE Sensex rose about 0.6% to 74,738 on Monday, rebounding from losses in the previous session as easing oil prices provided support to the benchmark, although crude prices remained elevated and heavy IPO activity capped broader gains. Oil prices remained a key focus for investors, with Brent crude trading around $101.6 a barrel after falling 2.2% in the previous session, but remaining at levels that could add to India’s inflation pressures and weigh on the country’s trade balance. The roughly $2.3 billion IPO of the National Stock Exchange was due to close on Monday, adding to competition for liquidity alongside several other new share offerings. 【最高法民刑交叉司法解釋明起施行】《最高人民法院關於審理涉及刑事犯罪的民事糾紛案件若干問題的規定》今天（21日）釋出，共計24條，9月22日起開始施行。最高法研究室主任周加海介紹，這部司法解釋將充分保障當事人合法權益貫穿始終，並從程式和實體兩個方面同時發力：一方面，堅持訴權的實質保護。比如，為避免動輒以涉及刑事犯罪為由干擾對民事案件的依法受理，導致當事人救濟受阻，司法解釋對哪些情況下應當作為民事案件受理以及如何審理、執行作出了明確規定。另一方面，依法公平公正保障實體權利。比如，糾正以往的涉及刑事的合同一概無效的觀點，明確涉刑合同的效力應當依照民法典的規定認定。又如，針對實踐中僵化適用擔保規則造成擔保人責任過重的問題，司法解釋對涉刑擔保合同效力認定作出專門規定，指引人民法院審慎認定擔保人過錯有無及大小，依法確定擔保責任。

Palm Oil Holds Near MYR 4,900 on Supply Concerns — Malaysian palm oil futures edged higher, hovering near MYR 4,900 per tonne after recent weakness, supported by firmer soyoils on China’s Dalian markets and expectations of tighter supplies. Indonesia’s B50 biodiesel mandate is set to divert more palm oil to domestic use, while El Niño risks could curb output in both Indonesia and Malaysia. Demand prospects also improved as palm oil imports by top consumer India rose 7% from July to 782,761 tonnes in August, the highest since February, with refiners replenishing stocks ahead of the festival season. Separately, Malaysia raised its October crude palm oil reference price but kept the export duty at 10%. However, a stronger ringgit tempered gains, along with weaker crude oil prices amid renewed efforts to ease Middle East tensions. Market focus now turns to a potential import tariff cut in India, with an official announcement expected soon that could sway demand for Malaysian palm oil.

### 2026-09-21 12:08:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Drops as Traders Monitor Middle East Diplomacy — European natural gas prices fell below €79/MWh on Monday after surging in the previous session, as traders monitored diplomatic efforts to end the Middle East conflict. President Donald Trump said he would probably be open to meeting Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian, who is expected to attend the United Nations General Assembly later this week, though Tehran has yet to respond to the remarks. Any progress toward de-escalation could ease concerns over prolonged LNG supply disruptions. However, supply risks remain elevated, with the conflict continuing to restrict LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz. European gas storage facilities were around 68.5% full, still below the seasonal average. The situation is further complicated by intense competition for LNG with Asia and ongoing maintenance work on Norway’s gas infrastructure. With the heating season approaching, the market remains highly sensitive to extended supply disruptions, increasing the risk of further price volatility.

Rupee Holds Gains as Oil Prices Ease — The Indian rupee hovered around 95.75 per dollar, extending gains from the previous session as softer oil prices, expected portfolio inflows and continued RBI intervention helped limit downside. Brent crude fell about 2% to $101.7 a barrel as hopes for a diplomatic solution to the Iran conflict and improving Saudi energy shipments eased supply concerns. The fully subscribed $2.3 billion NSE IPO could also attract foreign institutional inflows, while improved capital flows and RBI measures to encourage overseas FX deposits and borrowings have lifted reserves to about $781 billion. However, strong importer dollar demand and elevated oil prices remain headwinds. A firm dollar could add pressure, with Goldman Sachs now expecting the Federal Reserve to deliver another 25-basis-point hike in October.

Azerbaijan Consumer Prices Rebound in August — Consumer prices in Azerbaijan rose by 0.2% month-over-month in August 2026, rebounding from a 0.1% fall in the previous month. The recovery was largely driven by higher costs of food products, which increased by 0.3% after declining 0.4% in July, particularly for meat and fish goods. Non-food prices also rose (0.1% vs 0%), as higher costs of wallpaper, bricks, plasterboard, electrical equipment, personal hygiene products and jewelry outweighed lower prices of summer clothing, detergents and cleaning products, personal computers and tablets. Meanwhile, inflation remained steady for services at 0.2%, driven by increased prices for leisure tours and international passenger air transport services. On an annual basis, consumer prices rose by 5.7% in August, following a 5.8% gain in the preceding period.

【國家糧食和物資儲備局：推動糧食價格保持在合理水平】9月21日，國務院新聞辦公室舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，介紹守住管好“大國糧倉”，保障國家糧食安全有關情況，並答記者問。  國家發展和改革委員會黨組成員、國家糧食和物資儲備局局長劉煥鑫表示，“十五五”時期，將深入實施國家糧食安全戰略，堅持產量產能、生產生態、增產增收一起抓，提升糧食產購儲加銷協同保障能力，加強糧食購銷和儲備管理，推進糧食流通提質增效，促進糧食產需銜接、結構最佳化、品質提升，加快建立同我國大國地位相符的現代化糧食流通體系。劉煥鑫表示，抓好糧食購銷，促進糧食市場流通順暢、執行平穩。圍繞牢牢守住“種糧賣得出”“吃糧買得到”的底線，進一步活躍糧食購銷。堅持兩手結合，用好市場無形之手，大力營造良好環境，引導各類糧食經營主體積極入市；用好政府有形之手，組織好政策性收儲，發揮保供穩價的支撐作用。突出兩端發力，從供給端入手，推廣糧食訂單種植，強化糧食產後服務，提升糧食品質、適配市場需求；從需求端入手，加強糧食市場監測預警，用好儲備吞吐等手段，注重預調微調，穩定市場預期，推動糧食價格保持在合理水平。注重軟硬齊抓，完善糧食交易平臺體系，最佳化為農服務，加強產銷銜接，規範糧食市場秩序；加強糧食物流通道建設，強化核心樞紐和關鍵節點功能，增強“北糧南運”等糧食重要通道物流承載能力，提升跨區域調運效率。（大河財立方）

Rupiah Holds Near IDR 17,810 as BI Meeting Looms — The Indonesian rupiah was little changed on Monday, hovering near IDR 17,810 per U.S. dollar after a seven-session losing streak, as traders positioned for Bank Indonesia’s policy decision this week. The central bank will begin its two-day meeting Tuesday, after leaving key rates unchanged at 5.75% for a second straight month in August following 100bps of hikes since May. Governor Destry Damayanti recently underscored currency stability as her priority alongside monetary, macroprudential and payment-system measures. Meanwhile, softer oil prices, aided by diplomatic efforts in the Middle East, eased imported inflation concerns, though domestic pressures persisted after August’s annual inflation quickened to 3.19%. Fiscal risks also lingered as lawmakers reportedly debated revising the 3% deficit cap to allow more welfare and development spending. Globally, the dollar index held near 100.2 as investors looked to remarks from Fed officials this week for signals on the policy outlook.

Copper Gains for Fifth Straight Session — Copper futures climbed to around $6.65 per pound on Monday, extending gains for a fifth consecutive session, supported by signs of stronger demand and expectations of tighter global supply. The Yangshan premium, a key gauge of Chinese copper demand, rose to $121 a ton last week, reaching its highest level since November 2022. Copper also benefited from expectations of sustained long-term consumption driven by data centers and renewable energy projects. On the supply side, major producers including Chile, Indonesia and the Democratic Republic of Congo continued to face disruptions at several key mines due to operational issues, fatal accidents and severe storms, among other factors. Meanwhile, traders are closely watching US copper tariff policy and its potential effects on global trade flows.

【閒魚致歉】近日，據河北經視報道，閒魚平臺上至今仍暗藏涉黃產業鏈。針對此情況，9月21日，閒魚官方客服回應表示，“您提及的違規商品，我們在持續關注和管控。抱歉帶來了不好的體驗，閒魚在此真誠致歉。”客服解釋稱，平臺一直以來都禁止釋出各類違規資訊，並用多種手段識別、刪除違規商品。但仍有不法分子藉助各類“暗語”逃避平臺監管，平臺將不斷提升技術能力與稽核力度。針對使用者質疑為何仍有大量違規資訊未被處置，客服回應稱，使用者看到部分賣家可能違規卻未被處置，並不代表平臺認可該行為。“只要排查到違規屬實都會被處置，但因不是所有違規都直接封號，使用者可能無法感知處置過程。”目前，相關違規行為的排查工作仍在持續進行中。（中新經緯）

Wheat Climbs Nearly 2% — Wheat climbed nearly 2% toward $7.30 per bushel in mid-September, rebounding from a three-week low hit on September 18, as the latest round of US-China trade engagement fueled optimism ahead of the Trump-Xi summit. Officials from both countries, led by US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent and Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng, met over the weekend. Bessent described the discussions as “very successful,” while Lifeng said they were held in a “good atmosphere.” President Donald Trump and Chinese President Xi Jinping are set to meet in the US on September 24, with investors closely watching for signs of additional Chinese purchases of US agricultural products. The Trump administration has said Beijing pledged to purchase at least $17 billion of US farm exports, on top of a separate commitment to purchase 25 million tons of US soybeans yearly. Any further purchase commitments could therefore focus on other agricultural commodities, including wheat.

Japan 10-Year Yield Slips After BOJ Decision — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield slipped to around 2.98% after the Bank of Japan raised interest rates in a widely anticipated move, with two officials dissenting from the decision. The central bank increased its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1.25%, the highest level since 1995. Board members Asada and Sato opposed the hike, suggesting the BOJ may proceed with further increases at a slower pace than initially expected. Meanwhile, Governor Kazuo Ueda said the central bank remains committed to raising rates and adjusting the degree of monetary accommodation in response to changing economic conditions, while noting that accommodative conditions are expected to remain in place to support economic growth. Japanese markets will be closed from September 21 to 23 for national holidays.

### 2026-09-21 10:36:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield Hits Over 1-Year Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.67% on Monday, hitting its lowest level since July 2025, after the People’s Bank of China kept its benchmark lending rates unchanged for a 16th straight month at record lows. The one-year loan prime rate (LPR) was maintained at 3.0%, while the five-year LPR was held at 3.5%. The decision comes as policymakers face limited scope for further monetary easing after other major central banks shifted toward tighter policy, while the yield premium of 10-year US Treasuries over Chinese government bonds remained near a record high. Still, slowing economic growth, subdued inflation and weak credit demand continue to leave the door open to a rate cut before year-end. Meanwhile, investor attention remained focused on high-level US-China engagement after negotiators met on Sunday to pave the way for a closely watched summit between Presidents Donald Trump and Xi Jinping on September 24.

### 2026-09-21 10:36:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesia Stocks Retreat Further as Week Opens — Indonesian equities slipped 47 points, or 0.7%, to 6,392 in early Monday trade, extending previous losses as weakness in energy, infrastructure, and financials weighed on sentiment. Caution grew ahead of Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting later this week amid renewed rupiah pressure and signs of foreign outflows. Inflation risks also mounted on elevated oil prices and potential El Niño effects. Still, declines eased after the Finance Minister pledged refunds to taxpayers and businesses that overpaid, in line with regulations, while vowing to safeguard fiscal credibility and keep the deficit below 3% of GDP despite speculation of potential changes to the limit. In its main trading partner, China, the central bank held key lending rates steady for a 16th month ahead of a bilateral summit between President Xi Jinping and U.S. President Donald Trump on September 24. Notable laggards included Adaro Andalan (-3.3%), XLSmart Telecom (-2.0%), Indosat (-1.7%), and Telkom Indonesia (-1.6%).

【Shandong unveils fiscal‑financial package to spur domestic demand】Shandong provincial finance officials unveiled a fiscal‑financial package aimed at stabilizing expectations and boosting domestic demand by lowering costs, increasing investment and easing financing. Cost measures include loan interest subsidies for consumer and corporate credit; consumer loans will receive a 1 percentage‑point subsidy, capped at 5,000 yuan per person per bank per year. Investment steps deploy investment funds to steer social capital into digital and green consumption via long‑term, patient capital to support long‑cycle consumption industries. Policy and market‑access measures deepen a 'financial‑chain leader' mechanism across 19 strategic industrial chains, allocate dedicated re‑lending and rediscount quotas, accelerate financial direct‑to‑grassroots programs and set up direct financial links to address private‑sector financing difficulties.

【山東財政金融協同發力促內需 一攬子利好政策出爐】據山東釋出訊息，今天上午，山東省人民政府新聞辦公室舉行省人民政府政策例行吹風會，邀請省財政廳負責同志等解讀《關於財政金融協同促內需的若干措施》。穩定預期是擴內需、穩增長的重要保障，《若干措施》從降低成本、增加投入、最佳化環境三個維度入手，進一步提振市場信心。降低成本方面，從個人消費和企業融資兩端發力，透過貸款貼息等方式，提升消費和投資意願。比如，最佳化實施個人消費貸款貼息政策，對居民個人用於消費的貸款，給予1個百分點貼息，一個人在1家銀行1年可獲得最高5000元財政貼息。增加投入方面，發揮投資基金作用，引導帶動社會資本以市場化方式參與數字、綠色等新型消費領域，透過“長期資本”“耐心資本”投入，支援長週期消費產業發展。最佳化環境方面，深化19條標誌性產業鏈“金融鏈主”機制，安排再貸款、再貼現專用額度，推進“金融直達基層加速跑”，開展“民企金融直連解難題”等行動，促進消費和投資、供給和需求良性互動。

### 2026-09-21 10:24:03  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Trades Near 2023 Highs — Corn futures rose above $5.3 per bushel, trading near their highest level since mid-2023, supported by tightening global supplies and concerns over disruptions to Black Sea trade. The USDA now projects global corn production to fall short of consumption by nearly 30 million metric tons in 2026/27, which would mark the largest deficit in more than 30 years, with lower output among major exporters and continued disruptions to grain shipments from the Black Sea contributing to the shortfall. Brazil is also expected to divert more corn toward ethanol production, potentially reducing export availability, while lower planting growth could further tighten supplies. In the US, however, the advancing harvest is providing some pressure, with 8% of the crop harvested by September 13, ahead of the five-year average, while 57% was rated good to excellent, up from 56% a week earlier. US export sales have also weakened recently, tempering the bullish supply outlook.

【UBS cuts KOSPI 12-month target by nearly 10% to 8000 as macro headwinds worsen】UBS cut its 12-month KOSPI target to 8,000 from 8,800 in a note last Friday, citing higher rates, a stronger won and rising oil. The revision lowers UBS’s implied target P/E to 7x from 8x, reflecting deteriorating macro headwinds even as UBS still forecasts robust KOSPI EPS growth of 256% in 2026 and 38% in 2027. UBS said the index is likely to trade in a range until Q3–Q4 earnings provide clearer signals on earnings sustainability and shareholder returns. Key drivers cited: the 10-year Korean government bond yield has risen to 4.5% from 3.4% at the start of the year (≈+110bps), the Bank of Korea has raised rates twice since July, oil is trading above $100, and a stronger won is also a drag—UBS estimates a 1% won appreciation trims KOSPI earnings by roughly 1.1%.

Chicago Fed President Austan Goolsbee is scheduled to speak later Monday, followed by New York Fed President John Williams on Tuesday. Last week, the dollar index climbed more than 1% after the Fed raised interest rates and signaled at least one additional hike this year, while Chair Warsh reiterated the central bank’s commitment to bringing inflation under control. New Zealand Dollar Trades Near 10-Week Low — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.571 on Monday, near a ten-week low, as traders looked to a US-China summit later this week for indications on the state of relations between the world’s two largest economies. Among the key areas under discussion are an extension of the current tariff truce, which is set to expire on November 10, further tariff reductions, agricultural purchases, and AI. Any positive developments could improve global risk sentiment and support the kiwi, given that China is New Zealand’s largest trading partner. The kiwi has been under pressure since the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised its cash rate earlier this month but signalled a more gradual pace of future tightening. Still, markets largely expect the central bank to hike rates again in October, as the recent spike in oil prices has increased upside inflation risks. Meanwhile, a firmer US dollar, supported by expectations of more Fed rate hikes, has added to downward pressure on the kiwi.

China Stocks Gain as PBOC Stands Pat — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.4% to 3,925 on Monday, while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.8% to 13,743, as the People’s Bank of China kept its benchmark lending rates unchanged for a 16th consecutive month at record lows. The one-year loan prime rate (LPR) was maintained at 3.0%, while the five-year LPR was held at 3.5%. The decision underscored policymakers’ caution over the economic fallout from the conflict in the Middle East, although exports continued to benefit from robust AI-related demand. Still, slowing growth, subdued inflation and weak credit demand continue to provide scope for a rate cut before year-end. Meanwhile, attention remained focused on high-level US-China engagement after officials led by Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent and Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng met on Sunday to set the stage for a closely watched Trump-Xi summit. Notable gainers included Foxconn Industrial Internet (2.8%), WuXi AppTec Co (2.2%), Shennan Circuit (5%) and Chaozhou Three-Circle (5.9%).

Taiwan Stocks Hit 2-Month High on Tech Boost — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 268 points, or 0.6%, to 47,449, rising for the fourth straight session and hitting its highest level since June 22, when the index reached a record high, mainly buoyed by gains in electronic technology, commercial services, and energy minerals. Electronic technology rose 1.0%, with TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, gaining 0.6%. Meanwhile, MediaTek and ASE Technology Holding soared 5.4% and 4.1%, respectively. The broader index tracked a rise in US stock futures after the Dow Jones recorded a third straight losing week. However, traders monitored developments in US-China relations ahead of the Trump-Xi summit on September 24. Traders also anticipated Taiwan's export orders data for August after the closing bell, following a surge in July.

【產業鏈人士：特斯拉機器人團隊在長三角審廠，多家企業已獲訂單】特斯拉機器人團隊落地寧波審廠一事受到資本市場廣泛關注。9月21日，記者從產業鏈人士處獲悉，特斯拉機器人團隊已經於上週在拓普集團（601689）、三花智控（002050）、均勝電子（600699）等供應鏈企業開啟審廠工作。上述企業主要供給關節模組、執行器、精密結構件等核心機電部件。“在本次審廠前，這些企業都已經拿到了特斯拉人形機器人的訂單，只是或多或少的區別。審廠將對相關產線質量、合規等方面開展評估，透過後將很快開始生產。”上述人士提到。該名產業鏈人士還表示，審廠工作並非網傳的只在寧波展開，特斯拉該機器人團隊本次的走訪工作還覆蓋了杭州、上海等長三角地區的供應鏈企業。相關企業基本也都是特斯拉此前在汽車方面就已有合作的供應鏈企業。此前有訊息稱，特斯拉計劃在今年生產數千臺人形機器人。供應鏈審廠工作的開啟，意味著特斯拉人形機器人量產工作更進一步。（澎湃新聞）

### 2026-09-21 09:46:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #other #currency #Importance 1

Bitcoin Approaches January Highs — Bitcoin rose toward $82,000 in late September, approaching its highest level since January amid renewed institutional demand. US spot Bitcoin ETFs recorded $433 million in net inflows on September 18, led by Fidelity and BlackRock, reversing heavy outflows earlier in the week. Still, the funds ended the week with only $6.2 million in net inflows. The gain came despite the Federal Reserve raising rates by 25 bps last week and signaling further tightening, while US Treasury yields remained elevated. The divergence from the broader macro backdrop has raised interest in Bitcoin as a potential hedge against market and economic risks. Meanwhile, the Senate failed to advance the Clarity Act on September 15, while the SEC and CFTC moved ahead with crypto rulemaking under existing authority. The CFTC also submitted a crypto-market proposal to the White House for review.

【Westpac: Hawkish signals outweigh tactical reasons to wait at the Reserve Bank of Australia】Westpac chief economist Luci Ellis has brought forward her expectation for a Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) rate hike to September, saying the governor's comments satisfied the conditions set at the August meeting for further tightening. She acknowledged tactical reasons to delay — August monthly CPI due next day, easing Middle East tensions, potential fuel excise adjustments or a weak jobs print could complicate timing — but judged hawkish signals to carry greater weight. Westpac expects a divided vote at the RBA as members differ on trend productivity, labour supply and the degree of labour-market slack; the underemployment rate has risen sharply.

She added that, given the inflation backdrop, the risk is further tightening after September, though such a decision would be difficult.

### 2026-09-21 09:40:12  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Rally on Chip Gains — The benchmark KOSPI climbed more than 1% to around 6,995 on Monday, rallying for a second straight session amid strong global semiconductor momentum. Gains were led by Samsung Electronics and SK hynix, advancing nearly 4% and more than 1%, respectively, after US semiconductor stocks rallied, with the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index rising 2.78% on Friday. Additionally, South Korea's exports surged 78% year-on-year in the first 20 days of September, driven by a 259% jump in semiconductor shipments to $34.1 billion, highlighting continued strength in chip demand and a strong earnings outlook for chipmakers. Other notable performers included SK Square (4.1%), KB Financial Group (1.1%), Samsung C&T Corp (4.4%), Shinhan Financial Group (2.3%), and Hana Financial Group (1.7%). Meanwhile, Brent crude remained above $100 a barrel amid ongoing Middle East tensions, keeping inflation and broader macroeconomic concerns in focus.

【長鑫G5量產 通用DRAM工藝逼近國際一線】9月20日，長鑫科技宣佈第五代DRAM工藝平臺G5進入量產階段。此舉標誌著國產通用DRAM在理論工藝密度上已實質性追平國際一線水平，並顯著增強長鑫科技在主流市場的成本競爭力與定價話語權。G5平臺透過四重曝光技術在DUV體系下實現極限挖潛，使儲存位密度較上一代提升50%，每片晶圓裸片產出大幅增加。在當前儲存景氣上行、供給偏緊的視窗期，這一躍升為長鑫科技匯入國產旗艦手機供應鏈提供了堅實底氣。在DDR5、LPDDR5X等通用領域，長鑫科技已具備參與全球成本競爭的實力。此外，隨著基於G5平臺的擴產推進，國產半導體裝置佔比有望超過40%，將直接拉動刻蝕、薄膜沉積等環節的裝置採購需求，為上游核心供應商帶來確定的業績增量。（一財）

### 2026-09-21 09:25:42  #MKT News

【ChangXin G5 Enters Mass Production; Domestic DRAM Process Density Nears Global Leaders】Sept. 20 — ChangXin announced its fifth-generation DRAM platform G5 has entered mass production. G5, using quadruple-exposure DUV, raises bit density ~50% versus the prior generation and materially increases die-per-wafer, narrowing the process-density gap with international leaders. In the current memory upcycle amid tight supply, the capacity jump strengthens ChangXin’s cost competitiveness and pricing power, supporting entry into domestic flagship smartphone supply chains and enabling participation in global DDR5 and LPDDR5X cost competition. Continued G5-driven capacity expansion could lift the share of domestic semiconductor equipment to above 40%, directly increasing demand for etch and thin-film deposition tools and providing visible revenue upside for upstream suppliers.

### 2026-09-21 09:07:33  #市場 #國際

【美國柴油價格突破每加侖6.50美元 戰爭加劇全球燃料短缺】美國零售柴油價格首次突破每加侖6.50美元，延續了戰爭驅動的漲勢，這輪漲勢正波及整體經濟。據美國汽車協會(AAA)資料顯示，截至週六，全美平均油價已升至每加侖6.505美元。這一漲幅於9月加快 —— 本月迄今已上漲逾87美分 —— 價格幾乎每天都在上漲，並突破了2022年創下的峰值。美伊衝突已導致這種重要燃料的全球供應一直受到擠壓，經由關鍵的霍爾木茲海峽的運輸量仍受限制。與此同時，該地區的原油貨運量尚未完全恢復到戰前水平，這限制了全球其他地區煉油廠的燃料產量。此外，俄羅斯已禁止大部分燃料出口，並可能將這一限制措施延長至10月，而烏克蘭的無人機襲擊仍在持續打擊該國的煉油廠。週末，莫斯科煉油廠在一輪襲擊中遭到打擊，這是今年最大規模的夜間猛烈轟炸的一部分。大多數美國司機不會給自己的汽車加柴油。但這種用於卡車、農業機械、發電機、船隻、火車和家庭供暖系統的燃料幾乎涉及經濟的各個領域。隨著中期選舉臨近，創紀錄的柴油價格可能削弱美國總統唐納德·特朗普領導的共和黨在愛荷華州等農業州，以及緬因州等依賴家庭取暖用油的州所獲得的支援。儘管如此，卡車司機和消費者仍可稍感安慰，儘管他們可能並未意識到這一點。據總部位於華盛頓的智庫Institute for Progress稱，經通脹調整後，當前價格仍低於2022年的峰值。

---

【高盛：晶片業繁榮將增加韓國公司的現金】根據高盛的最早研究報告預測，隨著晶片行業的繁榮，韓國半導體公司預計到2026年將產生約150萬億韓元的淨現金，這相當於2026年韓國國內生產總值（GDP）的4.9%。高盛分析師團隊（由Irene Choi領銜）在這份報告中還指出了以下核心資料與趨勢：現金持續暴增：到2027年，韓國半導體公司的淨現金佔GDP的比重可能會進一步飆升至9.3%。海外利潤迴流：從歷史資料來看，這些晶片巨頭平均會將其海外關聯公司約60%的盈利匯回韓國國內。回饋本土經濟：高盛預計，到2027年，主要晶片公司在韓國國內支付的工資和股息將達到GDP的5.2%，這將創下現有歷史資料以來的最高水平，有望顯著刺激韓國本土的消費與經濟活力。

【新世界發展稱推進首個港資C-REIT】新世界發展在香港交易所釋出公告稱，有關建議分拆及證券投資基金於上海證券交易所獨立上市，上交所已於9月19日就建議分拆出具受理通知書。就建議分拆而言，證券投資基金管理人將設立證券投資基金作為公開募集商業不動產投資信託基金，於上交所進行證券投資基金髮售，預期規模約為38.2億元人民幣目前預期，基金份額總數的20%將由本集團認購。於建議分拆後，本集團將收取約32.4億元人民幣的所得款項淨額。資產為上海香港新世界大廈，由上海K11 Art Mall購物藝術中心及上海K11 ATELIER 新世界大廈組成。新世界發展CEO黃少媚在一份宣告中稱，NWD C-REIT將會是首個港資的內地商業不動產REIT。集團在中國內地擁有及經營眾多優質資產，包括杭州K11購物藝術中心及上海K11 ELYSEA專案將相繼落成。集團日後繼續將此類資產證券化，對回收資本、提升及改善流動性和去槓桿策略尤為重要。

### 2026-09-21 08:55:37  #其他

【智譜：MaaS平臺將上線資料內容不留存機制】智譜MaaS平臺宣佈，近期將上線資料內容不留存功能，為企業和開發者使用者提供更嚴格的資料隱私保護。資料內容不留存機制旨在減少模型呼叫內容在平臺側的持久化留存。任何使用者均可以申請開通，生效後，智譜MaaS平臺不會對使用者的輸入和輸出進行靜態儲存，資料僅用於完成當次模型呼叫。企業和開發者可透過MaaS控制檯提交開通申請，具體生效時間及適用範圍以平臺確認結果為準。智譜方面表示，這個功能並不意味著任何情形下都不留存資料。Batch API、File API等需要在平臺側持久化儲存任務或檔案的功能不在上述覆蓋範圍內，以及法律法規要求留存、為核查涉嫌違規或濫用行為的情況，平臺可能按有關要求留存相關資料30天及以上。（新浪科技）

Australian Stocks Slip at Start of Week — Australian shares fell 24 points, or 0.3%, to around 8,705 on Monday morning, marking a second subdued session as oil prices rose after Yemen's Houthis struck Saudi Arabia's capital, Riyadh. Locally, Reserve Bank of Australia Governor Michele Bullock struck a hawkish tone in parliament, warning that inflation remains stubbornly high and upside risks are materialising, keeping the risk of further rate hikes alive. Healthcare, non-energy minerals and industrials led declines, while gains in consumer durables, utilities and technology provided some offset. Two of the four major banks slipped, while Nextdc Ltd. Offshore Yuan Holds at Over 4-Year High — The offshore yuan held its gains around 6.69 per dollar on Monday, remaining at its strongest level since July 2022, as the People’s Bank of China kept its benchmark lending rates unchanged for a 16th consecutive month at record lows. The one-year loan prime rate (LPR) was held at 3.0%, while the five-year LPR remained at 3.5%. The decision comes as policymakers face limited room for further monetary easing as the yuan continues to strengthen and after other central banks shifted to rate hikes. However, current conditions continue to support the possibility of a rate cut before year-end, as economic growth slows, inflation remains subdued, and credit activity stays weak. Meanwhile, investor focus remained on high-level US-China engagement after officials from both countries met in New York on Sunday to prepare for a closely watched summit between Presidents Donald Trump and Xi Jinping on September 24.

【中信證券A股策略聚焦：年內最後的進攻視窗】中信證券研報稱，產業超級週期的後段，機構票見頂後通常都有一輪非機構票新高行情。目前的AI敘事、盈利週期所處的位置及全球貨幣環境，容易對機構票形成制約：1.AI算力投入沒有放緩，但市場對前沿模型廠商業化空間的預期在調整；2.全A非金融盈利在2026年三季度環比可能繼續上行，但同比增速頂點可能在今年四季度出現；3.美聯儲展現出控通脹的強硬姿態，至少在年內會營造偏緊的宏觀流動性氛圍。這些因素如果用機構視角和思維去看，無疑都會制約行情的高度。不過，如果站在短期情緒和籌碼週期的角度，疊加三季報的催化，市場具備活躍資金進攻新技術與新題材的土壤。建議積極把握年內最後的進攻視窗。

【中國水下機器人商業化應用提速 助力海洋牧場建設】在中國東北海濱城市遼寧省大連市的星海灣海域，一個個“方盒子”形狀的水下機器人緩緩下潛，上演了一場採捕海產品的實戰大比拼。 “透過人機協同，半個小時抓取了17個目標物，包括6個海螺、8個扇貝、2個海星、1個生蠔。”剛完成比賽的大連理工大學OurEDA隊成員張萬輝說。 9月17日至20日，2026年第十屆全國水下機器人大賽在遼寧大連舉行，來自大連理工大學、大連海事大學等國內高校、科研院所和相關領域企業的30支水下機器人隊伍參賽，比拼在真實近海環境下水下機器人的水下感知探測、水下定位通訊和水下智慧作業能力。中國擁有1.8萬餘公里大陸海岸線和1.4萬餘公里島嶼海岸線，海洋資源稟賦得天獨厚。近年來，中國多地積極推進“藍色糧倉”建設，已建設超過160個國家級海洋牧場，為“藍色經濟”的多元化發展提供了更多可能。 從海產品抓取到水下錨繩打撈，再到水下生態監測，海洋牧場企業在日常運營中的需求，意味著水下機器人產業擁有廣闊的市場前景。中國沿海省份正加速開發海洋水下機器人的應用場景，打通“產學研用金”全鏈條，推動海洋牧場產業向新發展。（新華社）

### 2026-09-21 08:27:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 2

Yen Consolidates Amid Holiday-Thinned Trading — The Japanese yen consolidated near 157 per dollar on Monday after falling more than 2% last week, with traders remaining on alert for possible intervention as Japan begins a three-day holiday. Tokyo has previously used periods of thin holiday liquidity to intervene in currency markets, while concerns were heightened by reports that the Bank of Japan conducted a rate check with market participants late Friday. The yen weakened sharply last week after the BOJ raised interest rates in a widely anticipated move, with two policymakers dissenting from the decision. Governor Kazuo Ueda said the BOJ remains committed to raising rates and adjusting the degree of monetary accommodation in response to changing economic conditions, while noting that accommodative conditions are expected to remain in place to support growth. The yen also came under pressure from expectations that Japan’s rate-hiking cycle could move more slowly than the Federal Reserve’s.

### 2026-09-21 08:26:01  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Nasdaq100 #Impact bullish #S&P500

【Galaxy Securities: Fed hikes do not alter AI's long-term productivity case】Galaxy Securities says Fed rate hikes do not change the core logic that AI will raise productivity. AI infrastructure build-out is boosting near-term demand for chips, power and related inputs, but over the medium-to-long term AI should lift total-factor productivity and expand supply capacity, producing structural disinflationary pressure that can offset inflation. Global AI capex remains on an upward trend and compute demand should continue to expand. Several Fed officials have signaled AI may raise the neutral rate, implying that even if nominal rates stay elevated the real policy rate could be relatively low. Galaxy concludes the AI sector’s growth resilience should sustain development in a higher-rate environment, and rate-driven valuation repricing may offer more reasonable entry points for firms with genuine earnings support.

【早期貿易資料顯示晶片熱潮推動下 韓國出口保持穩健增長勢頭】9月初韓國出口繼續保持穩健增長勢頭，全球對人工智慧(AI)的需求提振半導體產品出口，令高度依賴貿易的韓國經濟受惠。海關週一公佈的資料顯示，經工作日差異調整後，9月前20天的出口同比增長89.8%。8月同期初步報告的出口增幅為61.5%。未經調整的出口增長78.3%，進口增長26.7%，貿易順差接近230億美元。半導體依然是推動出口增長的引擎，晶片出口同比激增259.4%。石油產品出口增長47.8%，汽車出口增長9.3%。按目的地劃分，對中國出口增長了113.8%，對美國出口增長118%。對越南和歐盟的出口分別增長了44.8%和37%。資料顯示，韓國第三季度末的貿易勢頭依然強勁，進一步印證了韓國央行對經濟足夠強勁，能夠承受更高借貸成本的看法。韓國央行8月將基準利率上調0.25個百分點至3%，連續第二次會議加息。由於增長強於預期且潛在通脹頑固，決策者採取了先發制人的行動。
